      13.8   Well Control

          1.  Installation Requirements of BOP Stack and Control System:

       The distance between BOP control lines and relief lines shall be not less than
            1m. Steel Gap Bridge should be placed in such a way to protect lines from
            damage by vehicle crossing. Sundries shall not be packed on the control line
                shield, on which welding operations are forbidden.

       The air supply shall be separated from that of driller’s console and directly
             connected to the main air supply system, and shall be equipped with liquid/gas
               separators. Excessive bending and pinching on air hoses are forbidden.

       Electric power to BOP control system shall be directly from the main switch of
               the power board, and controlled by independent switches.

       Accumulators shall be in good condition, charged at its rated value, and ready
                for work at any time.

          2. Installation Requirements of Choke Lines and Kill Lines, and Relief Lines:

             a) Beside the drilling spool, one manual and one HCR gate valves on the side of
             chock manifold and two manual gate valves on the side of kill manifold shall
             be equipped, and the manual gate valve close to the spool shall be always open.

            b) The high-pressure co-flex hose shall be installed as straight as possible and
               fixed by anchor after installation.

             c) Relief lines (burning line and bleed line):

       No field welding is permitted on relief lines and the connecting style of relief
                lines must be flanged.
       The arrangement of relief lines shall take wind directions, dwellings, roads, oil
              tank areas, power lines and other facilities into consideration.
       While the two lines go in the same trend, the distance between the lines shall
             be at least 0.3m, and the two lines shall be fixed independently.
       The outlets of bleed line connecting with choke manifold shall be in a safe area
            which is at least 300m. The bleed line on the kill manifold side connecting with
                drilling spool shall be prepared and connected when necessary (about 70m from
               the wellhead).
       At every 10m~15m, turning points, and outlets, the lines shall be supported and
               fixed by cement foundations with anchor bolts or ground anchors. Hanging

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
             South Azadegan Oilfield Project

 Contract No:          AZNS-127 Re-Entry#01 Program               Page 205 of 229CCA/C/1401/27

               portions shall be supported firmly. If crossing the stumbling blocks such as
             brooks or ponds is needed, metal bridging supports are necessary.
       Pre-buried anchor bolts for cement foundations shall not be less than 20mm or
              3/4" in diameter, and greater than 0.5m in length.

             3. Pressure test value and requirements:

             a) A pressure test shall be conducted successfully where there is no pressure
               decline in 5 minutes for a low-pressure test and a minimum of 10 minutes for a
              high-pressure test.

            b) Prior to each high-pressure test, a low-pressure test with 200-300 psi shall be
               carried out to verify sealing capability at the circumstance of low pressure and
              avoid washing out sealing elements.

             c) High pressure test should be to the rated working pressure of ram preventers or
               the rated working pressure of the wellhead, whichever is the lowest, or to the
                rating required in DRILLING ROGRAM. Variable bore rams should be
                 initially pressure tested on the largest and smallest OD pipe sizes that may be
             used during the OPERATION. Annular preventers, with a joint of drill pipe
                 installed, should be tested to 70% of their working pressure. The high test for
              Kelly cocks,  kill lines and choke lines should be to their rated working
               pressures, each part in the choke manifold is tested respectively to its rated
             working pressures, the relief lines be pressure tested to no less than 1,450psi
             (10Mpa).

            d) When casing cup tester is used, the above test pressure should not exceed 80%
               of the internal yield pressure (burst pressure) of the exposed casing.

             e) The control system of BOP shall be pressure tested to 3,000psi (21Mpa) for its
                 reliability before putting into use.

               f) Except that the BOP control system is tested with hydraulic oil, other well
               control equipment shall be pressure tested by clear water.

            g) Testing tools

       All required sizes of the plug type testers, and cup type testers should be
               available for pressure testing purpose.

       When the cup type tester(s) is used for pressure test, the connection of the cup
                 testers has to be checked, to ensure  it will stand the load during the high
               pressures test. For surface casing, the cup tester is to be set in middle of the first
              casing joint in the top, so the first casing connection (between wellhead and

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
             South Azadegan Oilfield Project

 Contract No:          AZNS-127 Re-Entry#01 Program               Page 206 of 229CCA/C/1401/27

              casing head housing) below the well head is to be tested too while testing BOP
             and wellhead.

          4. Pressure test frequency:

      All ram type preventers, annular BOP, valves, choke and kill lines, choke manifold,
      Kelly valves and safety valves should be pressure tested at the following frequencies:

       Prior to installation where possible.

       After installation of wellhead and BOP assembly and prior to drilling.

       After the disconnection or repair of any pressure containment seal in the BOP
                stack, choke line, or choke manifold, but limited to the affected component.

       Prior to drilling into a confirmed or suspected high-pressure zone.

       At any time requested by the COMPANY REPRESENTATIVE.

       In any case, every one month or during the first trip after the one-month interval
              with a maximum interval of 45 days. This frequency is not required for shear
              rams. As a minimum, the shear rams are to be tested prior to resume drilling
              operation after 9-5/8” casing string has been set and adequate WOC.

          5. Work Requirement of Well Control Equipment:

       With shut-in casing pressure lower than 1000psi, while snubbing with annular
               preventer, drill tools shall have 18-deg shoulder tool joints, and the snubbing
               velocity shall not be greater than 0.2m/s.

       After shut off the annular preventer or ram preventer, with the shut-in casing
               pressure not greater than 2000psi, drill tools are allowed to pull up and down
              with the velocity less than 0.2m/s, however, rotating or tool joint passing
              through are forbidden.

       While drilling through pay zone and thereafter, BOP function (close-open) test
                for the BOPs shall be performed regularly.

       Kill manifolds shall not be used for drilling fluid fill-in.

       The maximum allowed shut-in casing pressure shall be remarkably indicated
            on choke manifolds.

       All gate valves of well control manifolds shall be tagged with serial numbers,
             and be indicated with on/Off.

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
             South Azadegan Oilfield Project

 Contract No:          AZNS-127 Re-Entry#01 Program               Page 207 of 229CCA/C/1401/27

       In case shear rams were used, after shearing the drill pipe with shear rams,
                retrieve the upper sheared drill pipe, lower a drill pipe joint with TIW valve
                installed into the hole and close pipe rams on it to prevent the possibility of
              shear ram’s failure.

          6. Management of Well Control Equipment:

       Well control equipment used by the drilling crew shall be managed and
              operated by designated personnel with clear post responsibilities.

       Well  site shall have specified component storeroom and rubber element
             storeroom with air conditioners respectively. The temperature of storerooms
                shall meet storage requirements of components and rubber elements.

       At least 1 set is available as back-up for every type ram assembly in the well
                  site. Ram preventer side door seals and ram rubber, every type gasket and bolt
                 etc. should be enough for backup.

          7. Killing Method and Shut-in Method:

       Driller's method is recommended for well killing.

       The hard Shut-in method is recommended for shutting in the well in Fahliyan
                reservoir.

          8. Well Control during drilling:

             a) Shut in Procedure in While on Bottom
       Position Kelly (if in use) and tool joint so that tool joints are clear of sealing
              elements.
       Shut off pumps, check for flow.
       If flow is noted, close the annular preventer and open remote-controlled choke
                line valve (HCR).
       If the well has been closed using the annular preventer, check space out (pipe
            rams vs. toll joint relative position) and close pipe rams and ram locks
       Bleed off pressure between pipe rams and annular preventer (if possible).
       Record shut-in drill pipe and casing pressures.
       Record volume gain and mark pits.
       Weigh mud in suction pits.
       Check blowout preventer and manifold for leaks.
       Check flowline and choke exhaust for flow.
       Check accumulator pressure.

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
             South Azadegan Oilfield Project

 Contract No:          AZNS-127 Re-Entry#01 Program               Page 208 of 229CCA/C/1401/27

            b) Shut in Procedure in While Tripping
       If a flow is detected, install full opening safety valve and close same.
       Position tool joints clear of the sealing elements and close annular preventer
             and open remote-controlled choke line valve (HCR).
       Make up grey valve (dart type inside BOP on full open safety valve). Then
           Make up Kelly or top drive (insert a pup joint or single between safety valve
             and top drive) and open safety valve.
       Record casing pressure.
       Check blowout preventer, stack, and manifold for leaks.
       Check valves for correct position.
       Check accumulator pressure.
       Begin stripping to bottom.

             c) Shut in Procedure for Empty Wells
       Try to RIH with drill pipe as more as possible if hole condition permits, and the
                  drill pipe must be installed a Kelly cock or safety valve.
       Close the annular preventer and open remote-controlled choke line valve
            (HCR).
       If the well has been closed using the annular preventer, check space out (pipe
            rams vs. tool joint relative position) and close pipe rams and ram locks
       Bleed off pressure between pipe rams and annular preventer (jf possible).
       Record casing pressure

          9. Well Control During Running Casing:

     The well control while running casing is important and critical, best practices shall be
      followed, including but not limited to:
       Change out pipe rams to casing rams prior to running casing into hydrocarbon
              bearing formation, if possible.
       The crossover between casing and drill pipe has to be available, and good
               practice is to make it up with a joint of DP and keep on V-door.

         10. Shut in Procedure During Logging:

     Cable shall be pull out of the hole immediately when overflow is found during logging.
        If pit gain exceeds the specified amount, the cable shall be cut immediately and treat the
      overflow in accordance with the shut-in procedure for empty wells. Continuing to pull
      the cable through the closed annular preventer is not recommended.

         11. Well Control During Testing:

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
             South Azadegan Oilfield Project

 Contract No:          AZNS-127 Re-Entry#01 Program               Page 209 of 229CCA/C/1401/27

       No difference to while DRILLING OPERATION, since either there is testing
                string in the well (same as Shut-in procedure while on bottom), or tripping the
                 string. (Same as shut-in procedure while tripping).
       Likely there is some crude in the testing string, and precaution has to be taken
              while circulating the crude out of hole during killing operations to ensure it is
              properly handled without causing environmental impact.
       To minimize the formation damage, the killing fluids used shall be weighted
               brine if possible, however in well control case when it occurs, killing well safely
                will take priority rather than protecting formation from damage on selection of
                killing fluids.

         12. Well Control During Stimulation:
       Similar to during testing.
       For any residual or alive acid to be circulated out during killing operations,
             measures have to be taken to treat the returned acid properly to avoid personnel
            from getting hurt, and without causing any environmental damage.

         13. Well Control Drills:

       Drills shall be conducted only when hole conditions and other rig activities permit. Drills
      should be scheduled so that crews can practice drills while on bottom, while tripping in
     and while out of the hole. Each operating shift shall perform at least one BOP drill under
       different situations per month.

         14. Basic Requirement of Well Control:
       For 13-5/8”, 10K BOP, BOP should be schemed as follows: upper rams 5”,
             middle rams are shear rams, lower rams is variable rams (2-7/8” to 5”). For
               easily connecting, armored fire-resistant 10000psi and ID 4-1/16" flexible
              hoses are recommended as kill/choke lines which connected with BOP directly.
       The BOP stack and choke manifold, kill line and choke line, HCR and manual
               gate valves, inside BOP (including kelly cock) have to be H2S trimmed and
              stand the working temperature from -10 degrees C (in case of doing N2 for
           DST) to the maximum up to 100 deg C (estimated highest temperature
               condition).
       The BOP  equipment  shall  be  visually  inspected,  function  tested, and
               hydrostatically pressure  tested during commissioning and Acceptance of
               Drilling Unit prior to Commencement Date, then upon installation on a
              wellhead, prior to drilling any hole section with a potential hydrocarbon bearing
                reservoir, after breaking any BOP connection (such as after installation of an

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
             South Azadegan Oilfield Project

 Contract No:          AZNS-127 Re-Entry#01 Program               Page 210 of 229CCA/C/1401/27

               additional wellhead section after running casing), and at least every 14-21 days
              while the BOP is installed on the Well.
       CONTRACTOR shall submit the current well control procedures manual of
         CONTRACTOR to COMPANY for review and approval by COMPANY.
              Valid IWCF Well Control Certificates (or IADC equivalent) must be held by
                  all tool pushers, Drillers (optional) at Drilling Supervisor level and Drillers and
               Assistant Drillers at Driller level, as further specified in CONTRACTOR
              Personnel HSE Requirements.
       The accumulator must be of sufficient capacity so that it is possible to close one
                (1) ram and the bag, open a ram and the bag, re-close a ram and the bag and
                retain 1200psi accumulator pressure.
       The “OPEN” and “CLOSED” operating positions of BOP activators of the
              rams, annular, and remote actuated valves must be clearly identified on the BOP
              master control panel and remote panels, in English in addition to the native
             language of the majority of the senior drilling crew.
       Shear-ram BOP activator must have a flap-cover to ensure only pre-meditated
             and deliberated activation and to prevent accidental WORK.
       All lines and connections in the choke and kill manifold, standpipe manifold,
             choke and kill lines and high-pressure mud lines should be welded hard-piping
              with weld-on FMC Weco “hammer-lug” 1502 unions (or Western equivalent,
             such as Anson) or weld-on API flanges. The well control system should not
              include any threaded screw-on hammer-lug unions, flanges, or other threaded
             screw-on connections.
       All  adapter or spacer spools between  the wellhead and BOP must be
             manufactured in accordance with API 6A, and must be complete with all
               required studs, nuts, and ring gasket seals for both lower and upper connections;
           minimum spare parts should include two (2) OEM spare studs complete with
              nuts and two spare OEM ring gasket seals for both bottom and top connections.
       There should be a wide-bore gas vent line from the “poor-boy” degasser; the
                line and line outlet must be securely anchored, the line outlet must be a safe
               distance from the well cellar, rig floor, choke manifold, and mud logging unit.
       Well Control Equipment Welding: All welds in the well control system must
             be NDT inspected and records maintained of all welding and weld inspections,
              including choke and kill manifold, standpipe manifold, mud pump discharge
              manifold, choke and kill lines.
       Well Control Rig Floor Information: The following must be posted on the rig
                floor (in the Driller’s shack  / “dog-house”): Well Shut-In Procedure, Well
              Information Sheet (showing casing and hole sizes, depths, and such like), Kill
              Sheet (including most recent slow circulation rate pressures, formation integrity

────────────────────────────────────────────
────────────────────────────────────────────
             South Azadegan Oilfield Project

 Contract No:          AZNS-127 Re-Entry#01 Program               Page 211 of 229CCA/C/1401/27

               leak-off test information and MAASP, present mud weight, present drill string
             and well volumes, and such like).
       The derrick substructure clear height (rotary table to ground level) needs to be
            enough for fitting in above BOP configuration (including a tubing head and a
                bell nipple). This need to be double checked in rig tender process. According to
               the calculation of height of BOP stack, the substructure clear height is at least
            9 m.
       In the case if drilling 6-1/8" hole becomes necessary, or using 3-1/2" drill pipe
                for the cleanup run (including drill 7" shoe track), then taper drilling string (5"
         DP + 3-1/2" DP) is to be run, so a pair variable ram be installed in the single
            ram preventer. The defect installing variable ram in single ram preventer is that
               the ram may be damaged and the string fall in hole bottom due to the ram
             hanging up too much weight when the drill pipe be cut. If the pipe ram be
                installed in the single ram preventer and the variable ram be installed in the
             double ram preventer, it would be very dangerous due to the variable ram failure
                in frequently using.

      13.9  H2S Monitoring and Prevention Measure

    As per PPL/PEDEC SOW South Azadegan oilfield contains H2S bearing formations,
      precautions shall be taken in place especially in 12-1/4” & 8-1/2” hole section (for
      typical Fahliyan vertical well) while drilling, completion and well testing operation.
     H2S can be released any time while drilling, completion and well testing operation,
      therefore:
       The Rig shall be prepared for H2S (Training, procedures and protective gear).
       Make sure everyone on the Rig should be made aware of the hazard presented
              by H2S during their induction when they first arrive on the rig.
       The mud shall be treated to protect the drill pipe and the casing.
       All training program shall be recorded and reported.
       H2S emergency drill must be conducted frequently.
       Adequate sensors and alarms must be installed for the scope of monitoring
                 the H2S releases.

────────────────────────────────────────────
             South Azadegan Oilfield Project

  Contract No:          AZNS-127 Re-Entry#01 Program               Page 212 of 229 CCA/C/1401/27
